# Constants for tools/constants.py: the names of action, command, flag and effect numbers.
# Each line's comment (up to a ;) goes into src/efz.h with it (tools/constants.py declare).
bound 0x31 kLastFreeAction  # the last of the actions a player moves freely in (standing, walking, jumping...); hit reactions follow
bound 0x32 kFirstHitReaction  # the first hit reaction (0x32-0x95)
bound 0x95 kLastHitReaction  # the last hit reaction; guard follows
bound 0x96 kFirstGuard  # the first guard reaction (0x96-0x9c)
bound 0xc7 kLastSharedAction  # the last action every character shares
bound 0xc8 kFirstCharacterAction  # the first of the character's own actions: normals, specials, supers, objects
action CPlayer 0x0 kStand  # player.cpp MoveOnGround with no input; the default case loops the animation
action CPlayer 0x1 kWalkForward  # player.cpp left/right toward facing; akane_vf6.cpp vx 0.7
action CPlayer 0x2 kWalkBack  # player.cpp; akane_vf6.cpp vx -0.7
action CPlayer 0x3 kCrouch  # player.cpp down held, after 0x8
action CPlayer 0x4 kJump  # player.cpp up, no direction; akane_vf6.cpp takes off at frame 3 (vy -3.3, gravity 0.054), lands in 0xd; a run's momentum (m_dashMomentum) adds vx
action CPlayer 0x5 kJumpForward  # player.cpp; akane_vf6.cpp vx 0.7 (1.1 from a run)
action CPlayer 0x6 kJumpBack  # player.cpp; akane_vf6.cpp vx -0.7
action CPlayer 0x7 kStandUp  # player.cpp down released while crouching; akane_vf6.cpp -> 0; also the end of standing no-push hits 0x48/0x49
action CPlayer 0x8 kCrouchDown  # player.cpp down pressed while standing; akane_vf6.cpp -> 0x3
action CPlayer 0x9 kFall  # akane_vf6.cpp falling after a jump's animation, an air dash, air 22C, recoil guard in the air; lands in 0xd
action CPlayer 0xa kSuperJump  # player.cpp up with m_command 3, which no ReadCommand produces: unreachable; Kanna and Mayu reuse 0xa for a state of their own
action CPlayer 0xb kSuperJumpForward  # player.cpp m_command 4, unreachable; data like 0x5 with a 19998-tick frame
action CPlayer 0xc kSuperJumpBack  # player.cpp m_command 5, unreachable
action CPlayer 0xd kLand  # akane_vf6.cpp landing from any jump or fall (sound 9) -> 0x7, or 0x3 holding down
action CPlayer 0xe kAirJump  # akane.cpp command 6 in the air with m_airActions left; akane_vf6.cpp vy -3.2
action CPlayer 0xf kAirJumpForward  # akane.cpp the same holding forward; vx 0.7
action CPlayer 0x10 kAirJumpBack  # akane.cpp holding back; vx -0.9
action CPlayer 0x2d kHudName  # scene3_draw.cpp the name plate under the life bar (Akane's reads "AKANE SATOMURA"); 0x2e holds the same sprite and is never drawn
action CPlayer 0x2f kResultLoserFall  # scene5.cpp the loser drops in from (480, -240) on the result screen; akane_vf6.cpp falls, lands at frame 2 (sound 7) and lies
action CPlayer 0x30 kHudPortrait  # scene3_draw.cpp the face beside the life bar
action CPlayer 0x31 kOffscreenMarker  # player.cpp a speech-bubble face drawn at the screen's bottom edge when the player is below the screen (unless m_hideOffscreenMarker)
action CPlayer 0x32 kHitStandHighLight  # scene3.cpp a standing defender, light attack; sprite: head snapped back; akane_vf6.cpp pushed back (vx -1.1 * (2 - proration)), gauge +10
action CPlayer 0x33 kHitStandHighMedium  # flagsAA 0x10; akane_vf6.cpp vx -1.5, gauge +15
action CPlayer 0x34 kHitStandHighHeavy  # flagsAA 0x20; akane_vf6.cpp vx -1.8, gauge +20
action CPlayer 0x35 kHitStandLowLight  # flagsAA 8 (2A, 2B...): sprite doubled over; akane_vf6.cpp as 0x32
action CPlayer 0x36 kHitStandLowMedium  #
action CPlayer 0x37 kHitStandLowHeavy  #
action CPlayer 0x38 kHitCrouchLight  # scene3.cpp a crouching defender (flagsB0 2); akane_vf6.cpp -> 0x3 at the end
action CPlayer 0x39 kHitCrouchMedium  #
action CPlayer 0x3a kHitCrouchHeavy  #
action CPlayer 0x3b kHitAir  # scene3.cpp the default air reaction (also both players on a double KO,); akane_vf6.cpp vx m_hitVx, vy = vy*0.75 + m_hitVy kept within -2..-0.5, gravity 0.032; lands in 0x61/0x62
action CPlayer 0x3c kHitAirHigh  # akane_vf6.cpp as 0x3b, rising speed capped at 4
action CPlayer 0x3d kHitAirHigher  # akane_vf6.cpp capped at 6
action CPlayer 0x3e kHitAirCarry  # akane_vf6.cpp vy*0.75 + m_hitVy, no cap
action CPlayer 0x3f kHitAirLaunch  # akane_vf6.cpp vy = m_hitVy outright (the usual launcher/sweep reaction, 440 attack frames)
action CPlayer 0x40 kHitAirWallBounce  # akane_vf6.cpp 0x3b that turns into 0x46 at a screen edge
action CPlayer 0x41 kHitAirHighWallBounce  # akane_vf6.cpp 0x3c with the wall bounce
action CPlayer 0x42 kHitAirCarryWallBounce  # akane_vf6.cpp 0x3e with the wall bounce
action CPlayer 0x43 kHitAirLaunchWallBounce  # akane_vf6.cpp 0x3f with the wall bounce
action CPlayer 0x44 kHitBlownAwayWallBounce  # akane_vf6.cpp flies in a straight line at (m_hitVx, m_hitVy) every tick, no gravity, -> 0x46 at an edge; its frame has flagsAA 0x400 (no recovery)
action CPlayer 0x45 kHitBlownAway  # akane_vf6.cpp the same without the bounce (supers' finishers, 35 attackers)
action CPlayer 0x46 kHitWallBounce  # akane_vf6.cpp bounces off the wall (vx 0.5, vy -2.5, sound 0x1f, effect 0), +120 untech time once
action CPlayer 0x47 kHitAirHeavy  # akane_vf6.cpp vy = m_hitVy with gravity 0.072 instead of 0.032: slammed down
action CPlayer 0x48 kHitStandHighHold  # akane_vf6.cpp no movement, 72 ticks, -> 0x7; head-hit pose; used by multi-hit supers (Kaori 0x12c-0x13a) to hold the opponent in place
action CPlayer 0x49 kHitStandLowHold  # akane_vf6.cpp the same, doubled-over pose
action CPlayer 0x4a kHitCrouchHold  # akane_vf6.cpp the same crouching, -> 0x3
action CPlayer 0x4b kHitStandHighPull  # akane_vf6.cpp slides toward the attacker (vx +2.2 slowing by 0.06), head-hit pose; Minagi's, Mio's, Mishio's pulling attacks
action CPlayer 0x4c kHitStandLowPull  # akane_vf6.cpp doubled-over pose
action CPlayer 0x4d kHitCrouchPull  # akane_vf6.cpp crouching, -> 0x3
action CPlayer 0x51 kHitBurn  # akane_vf6.cpp spawns 0x1f3 (blue flames over the player), drifts down and lands in a knockdown; hands-to-face pose; its frame has flagsAA 0x400
action CPlayer 0x52 kHitShock  # akane_vf6.cpp frames 0-3 alternate the sprite with a black silhouette in a lightning outline (8 loops, sound 0x1e), then launched by m_hitVx/m_hitVy; flagsAA 0x400
action CPlayer 0x53 kEncased  # akane_vf6.cpp the sprite becomes a jagged cream-coloured block (shared palette, sprite 422); spawns 0x1f0 once, hops back (sound 0x21) and breaks free (frames 63-61) to 0; Kano's 421 shots, Shiori's 236
action CPlayer 0x54 kEncased2  # akane_vf6.cpp identical code and data to 0x53; other attackers (Kano 0x1a7/0x1ac, Nayuki, Shiori)
action CPlayer 0x55 kEncasedAir  # akane_vf6.cpp the block lying (sprite 423) launched by m_hitVx/m_hitVy, gravity 0.04, knockdown on landing
action CPlayer 0x56 kEncasedSpin  # akane_vf6.cpp the block tumbling (sprites 423-429), gravity 0.06, knockdown on landing
action CPlayer 0x57 kFallInDarkness  # akane_vf6.cpp the player's sprite is blank and its screen-space object 0x1ef shows it falling; entered only from Kaori's LV4, which darkens the screen (CGame::unk1420e)
action CPlayer 0x60 kWakeUp  # akane_vf6.cpp getting up after 0x63 (invulnerable, throw-protected), -> 0x0/0x3; 22D during it (command 0x35) starts 0xa2 only for Mizuka B
action CPlayer 0x61 kKnockdownHittable  # akane_vf6.cpp hitting the ground (shake, effect 3, sound 7) after an attack with flagsAA 0x800 (m_landHittable): its frames have no flagsB0 0x20, so it can be hit; -> 0x63
action CPlayer 0x62 kKnockdown  # akane_vf6.cpp the usual ground hit after an air reaction, invulnerable (flagsB0 0x63); -> 0x63
action CPlayer 0x63 kLyingDown  # akane_vf6.cpp lies 100 ticks then 0x60, or for good when KO'd; supers drop opponents straight into it
action CPlayer 0x64 kThrownStandHigh  # throw pose set on the opponent and moved around by throws (akane_vf6.cpp etc.); sprite of 0x32's first frame; hittable

flag PatternFrame::flagsAA 0x1 kAttackGuardStand  # may be guarded standing; without it the attack is a low (player.cpp can't be guarded standing). On a frame with flagsB0 0x200 it says which heights the frame's guard point covers
flag PatternFrame::flagsAA 0x2 kAttackGuardCrouch  # may be guarded crouching; without it an overhead. 2A is 0xa (crouch-only + body hit), air attacks 0x25 (no 2)
flag PatternFrame::flagsAA 0x4 kAttackGuardAir  # may be guarded in the air; without it an air guard fails unless the recoil-guard window is open
flag PatternFrame::flagsAA 0x8 kAttackBodyHit  # a standing defender takes 0x35-0x37 (doubled over) instead of 0x32-0x34 (head snapped back); on 2A/2B and some others
flag PatternFrame::flagsAA 0x10 kAttackMedium  # strength: +1 to the hit reaction (0x33) and the guard reaction (0x97)
flag PatternFrame::flagsAA 0x20 kAttackHeavy  # strength: +2 (0x34, 0x98); neither bit = light
flag PatternFrame::flagsAA 0x40 kAttackThrow  # tests the defender's push box (box90) instead of its hurtboxes; whiffs on m_throwProtect (unk130 > 0); ignores armor; never hits objects; on 0xdc/0xf0 (throws) and command throws
flag PatternFrame::flagsAA 0x80 kAttackUnblockable  # CanGuard fails and recoil guard too; throws and some supers
flag PatternFrame::flagsAA 0x100 kAttackIgnoresArmor  # beats armor (flagsB0 4 / m_armorTime, scene3.cpp), the parry frames (flagsB0 0x800/0x1000,) and an object reflect (flagsB0 0x400,)
flag PatternFrame::flagsAA 0x200 kAttackUnused200  # set on 1237 frames, never tested by the code
flag PatternFrame::flagsAA 0x400 kAttackNoAirRecovery  # sets the defender's m_noAirRecovery; also on a hit reaction's own frame (0x44, 0x51, 0x52): no air recovery during it and no untech bar
flag PatternFrame::flagsAA 0x800 kAttackLandHittable  # sets m_landHittable: the defender lands in 0x61, which can still be hit, instead of 0x62
flag PatternFrame::flagsAA 0x1000 kAttackUnused1000  # set on 142 frames (Ayu 0x13b-0x13d, Mio 0x1a2 ...), never tested
flag PatternFrame::flagsAA 0x2000 kAttackCounterHit  # with flagsB0 0x2000 on the defender's frame: a counter hit; sets m_counterHit (scene3.cpp, never read); an object's counter hit doubles the untech time
flag PatternFrame::flagsB0 0x1 kStateStanding  # posture: hit as standing (0x32-0x37, scene3.cpp); 0x1|0x2 = airborne (0x3b,)
flag PatternFrame::flagsB0 0x2 kStateCrouching  # posture: hit as crouching (0x38-0x3a, scene3.cpp)
flag PatternFrame::flagsB0 0x4 kStateArmor  # hits do damage but cause no reaction (tint 0xdf, m_attackResult 7) unless flagsAA 0x100 or 0x40; data: unused patterns 0x91-0x95, 0xae/0xaf, a few moves
flag PatternFrame::flagsB0 0x8 kStateCancellable  # a move may start on this frame: tested by every StartCommand before each move (1002 sites, akane.cpp); idle states have it
flag PatternFrame::flagsB0 0x10 kStateCanGuard  # the player may guard here; a hit landing on such a frame starts a new combo. On an object's frame: a hit on it doesn't use up the attacker's hit
flag PatternFrame::flagsB0 0x20 kStateInvulnerable  # not hit at all: knockdown landing 0x62, lying 0x63, wake-up 0x60, supers' startup
flag PatternFrame::flagsB0 0x40 kStateThrowProtect  # keeps m_throwProtect at 27: jump start (4-6), guard (0x96-0x9c), knockdowns, wake-up
flag PatternFrame::flagsB0 0x80 kStateUnused80  # one frame (Mai 0xcf), never tested
flag PatternFrame::flagsB0 0x200 kStateGuardPoint  # with the frame's own flagsAA 1/2/4: guards attacks of those heights without leaving the move (chip damage, hitstop, guard gauge, m_attackResult 2; scene3.cpp); Akiko/Ayu 0xd3, Misaki, Mishio's supers
flag PatternFrame::flagsB0 0x400 kStateReflect  # an object's attack on this frame is cancelled (both players' m_attackResult = 6) unless flagsAA 0x100; set on no frame in 4.02's data
flag PatternFrame::flagsB0 0x800 kStateParryLow  # parries attacks that can be guarded crouching (flagsAA 2) without 0x100: both m_attackResult = 6, the attacker loses the hit; the counter moves of Kaori, Misaki, Misuzu, Sayuri check for 6
flag PatternFrame::flagsB0 0x1000 kStateParryHigh  # the same for attacks that can be guarded standing (flagsAA 1)
flag PatternFrame::flagsB0 0x2000 kStateCounterHittable  # the startup and active frames of attacks: a hit with flagsAA 0x2000 is a counter hit. On an object's frame: a hit on the object hits its owner
